### [1] [Mapping the Landscape of Affective Extended Reality: A Scoping Review of Biodata-Driven Systems for Understanding and Sharing Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11710)
*Zhidian Lin,Allison Jing,Ziyuan Qu,Fabio Zambetta,Ryan M. Kelly*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对82篇论文进行范围综述，提出"情感扩展现实"概念，分析XR系统中使用生物数据理解情感的技术、交互方法和评估方式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI文献中存在许多使用生物数据理解情感的XR系统，但这些系统尚未被系统性地整合和映射，缺乏对情感XR领域的整体认识

Method: 对82篇探索生物数据、情感和XR交叉领域的论文进行范围综述，分析这些系统中使用的技术、交互技术和评估方法

Result: 绘制了当前情感XR的现状图，揭示了情感共享目标的多样性，展示了HCI研究者如何探索XR生物反馈系统中的交互流程设计，突出了关键设计维度和理解情感的挑战

Conclusion: 讨论了情感共享中未充分利用的方法，并强调了情感XR未来研究的机会，为这一新兴领域提供了系统性的框架和方向

Abstract: This paper introduces the notion of affective extended reality (XR) to characterise XR systems that use biodata to enable understanding of emotions. The HCI literature contains many such systems, but they have not yet been mapped into a coherent whole. To address this, we conducted a scoping review of 82 papers that explore the nexus of biodata, emotions, and XR. We analyse the technologies used in these systems, the interaction techniques employed, and the methods used to evaluate their effectiveness. Through our analysis, we contribute a mapping of the current landscape of affective XR, revealing diversity in the goals for enabling emotion sharing. We demonstrate how HCI researchers have explored the design of the interaction flows in XR biofeedback systems, highlighting key design dimensions and challenges in understanding emotions. We discuss underused approaches for emotion sharing and highlight opportunities for future research on affective XR.

</details>


### [2] [Methodological Variation in Studying Staff and Student Perceptions of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11158)
*Juliana Gerard,Morgan Macleod,Kelly Norwood,Aisling Reid,Muskaan Singh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 比较学生与教职员工对AI感知的不同研究方法，发现单一数据源的不同分析方法会产生不同结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究通常使用单一量化或质化方法，或混合方法来比较学生和教职员工对AI的感知，但不同方法可能产生不同结果，需要系统比较这些方法

Method: 收集两种质化数据：独立评论和结构化焦点小组；对每种数据源进行两种分析：情感和立场分析（测量整体积极性/消极性），以及词云分析和主题分析（提供详细内容分析）

Result: 不同分析方法对同一数据源会产生不同结果，整体积极性/消极性测量与详细内容分析呈现不同图景

Conclusion: 研究方法的选择会影响研究结果，这对机构决策和先前研究比较具有重要启示，需要谨慎选择分析方法

Abstract: In this paper, we compare methodological approaches for comparing student and staff perceptions, and ask: how much do these measures vary across different approaches? We focus on the case of AI perceptions, which are generally assessed via a single quantitative or qualitative measure, or with a mixed methods approach that compares two distinct data sources - e.g. a quantitative questionnaire with qualitative comments. To compare different approaches, we collect two forms of qualitative data: standalone comments and structured focus groups. We conduct two analyses for each data source: with a sentiment and stance analysis, we measure overall negativity/positivity of the comments and focus group conversations, respectively. Meanwhile, word clouds from the comments and a thematic analysis of the focus groups provide further detail on the content of this qualitative data - particularly the thematic analysis, which includes both similarities and differences between students and staff. We show that different analyses can produce different results - for a single data source. This variation stems from the construct being evaluated - an overall measure of positivity/negativity can produce a different picture from more detailed content-based analyses. We discuss the implications of this variation for institutional contexts, and for the comparisons from previous studies.

</details>


### [3] [BIRD: A Museum Open Dataset Combining Behavior Patterns and Identity Types to Better Model Visitors' Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11160)
*Alexanne Worm,Florian Marchal,Sylvain Castagnos*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过眼动追踪设备收集博物馆参观者的综合数据，构建了一个包含人口统计、行为轨迹、注视数据和反馈信息的开放数据集，旨在解决文化遗产领域数据不足的问题，为个性化参观路径推荐提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能领域普遍存在数据不足的问题，特别是在文化遗产领域，开放数据集相对有限，且现有数据通常是针对特定模型评估的临时性数据，无法全面建模参观者的整体体验。为了克服这一缺陷，需要获取关于参观者、参观体验和反馈的全面详细信息。

Method: 研究于2019年2月至3月进行，为51名参与者配备眼动追踪眼镜，让他们自由探索博物馆的3层楼面（包含400多件艺术品），平均参观时间为57分钟。在此基础上构建了一个开放数据集，包含：1) 上下文数据（人口统计、偏好、参观习惯、动机、社交背景等）；2) 行为数据（时空轨迹、注视数据）；3) 反馈数据（满意度、疲劳度、喜欢的艺术品、口头反馈等）。

Result: 分析能够重现文献中发现的大多数参观者特征，并复制了Veron和Levasseur的参观者档案。该数据集最终将能够通过个性化以下方面来提高博物馆推荐路径的质量：兴趣点数量、在不同兴趣点花费的时间、以及根据参观者兴趣水平提供的信息量。

Conclusion: 该研究创建了一个全面的博物馆参观者数据集，解决了文化遗产领域数据不足的问题。该数据集不仅能够重现现有的参观者分类模型，还为未来开发个性化博物馆参观路径推荐系统提供了重要数据基础，有望改善参观体验的质量和个性化程度。

Abstract: Lack of data is a recurring problem in Artificial Intelligence, as it is essential for training and validating models. This is particularly true in the field of cultural heritage, where the number of open datasets is relatively limited and where the data collected does not always allow for holistic modeling of visitors' experience due to the fact that data are ad hoc (i.e. restricted to the sole characteristics required for the evaluation of a specific model). To overcome this lack, we conducted a study between February and March 2019 aimed at obtaining comprehensive and detailed information about visitors, their visit experience and their feedback. We equipped 51 participants with eye-tracking glasses, leaving them free to explore the 3 floors of the museum for an average of 57 minutes, and to discover an exhibition of more than 400 artworks. On this basis, we built an open dataset combining contextual data (demographic data, preferences, visiting habits, motivations, social context. . . ), behavioral data (spatiotemporal trajectories, gaze data) and feedback (satisfaction, fatigue, liked artworks, verbatim. . . ). Our analysis made it possible to re-enact visitor identities combining the majority of characteristics found in the literature and to reproduce the Veron and Levasseur profiles. This dataset will ultimately make it possible to improve the quality of recommended paths in museums by personalizing the number of points of interest (POIs), the time spent at these different POIs, and the amount of information to be provided to each visitor based on their level of interest.

</details>


### [4] [DiSCoKit: An Open-Source Toolkit for Deploying Live LLM Experiences in Survey Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11230)
*Jaime Banks,Jon Stromer-Galley,Samiksha Singh,Collin Capano*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DiSCoKit是一个开源工具包，用于通过JavaScript支持的在线调查平台（如Qualtrics）部署实时大型语言模型体验，解决人机交互研究中AI刺激部署的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互的社会科学研究需要向参与者提供实时LLM体验，但技术挑战（从聊天数据记录到AI行为操控）阻碍了基于调查的AI刺激部署。

Method: 开发了DiSCoKit开源工具包，支持通过JavaScript调查平台部署基于Microsoft Azure门户等提供的实时LLM体验，包含架构设计、操作流程和部署功能。

Result: 成功创建了一个能够克服技术障碍的工具包，使研究人员能够在在线调查环境中部署和控制实时AI交互，支持实验设计需求。

Conclusion: DiSCoKit为人机交互研究提供了实用的技术解决方案，扩展了基于调查的AI刺激部署能力，同时承认其部署可能性和局限性。

Abstract: Advancing social-scientific research of human-AI interaction dynamics and outcomes often requires researchers to deliver experiences with live large-language models (LLMs) to participants through online survey platforms. However, technical and practical challenges (from logging chat data to manipulating AI behaviors for experimental designs) often inhibit survey-based deployment of AI stimuli. We developed DiSCoKit--an open-source toolkit for deploying live LLM experiences (e.g., ones based on models delivered through Microsoft Azure portal) through JavaScript-enabled survey platforms (e.g., Qualtrics). This paper introduces that toolkit, explaining its scientific impetus, describes its architecture and operation, as well as its deployment possibilities and limitations.

</details>


### [5] [Same Feedback, Different Source: How AI vs. Human Feedback Shapes Learner Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11311)
*Caitlin Morris,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨反馈来源（AI vs 人类助教）如何影响学习者参与度和评价，发现尽管反馈内容相同，人类助教来源的反馈能显著提升学习者的时间和精力投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI与人类教师共同参与教育，理解学习者对反馈来源的归因效应对于设计有效的混合AI-人类教育系统至关重要。需要探究学习者如何根据反馈来源（AI或人类）来调整他们的参与行为。

Method: 设计了一个创意编程界面，在控制反馈内容相同的情况下隔离来源归因：所有参与者接收相同的LLM生成反馈，但一半被告知反馈来自AI，另一半被告知来自人类助教（TA）。通过比较两组参与者的行为数据和评价来研究来源效应。

Result: 1. 反馈来源显著影响参与度：TA条件下的学习者尽管接收相同反馈，但花费的时间和精力显著更多（效应量d = 0.88-1.56）。2. 评价机制不同：AI归因反馈的评分由对AI的先前信任预测（r = 0.85），而TA归因反馈的评分由感知真实性预测（r = 0.65）。

Conclusion: 反馈来源不仅影响学习者对反馈的评价方式，还显著影响他们的参与行为。这对混合教育系统设计有重要启示：需要考虑如何呈现反馈来源以优化学习效果，可能需要策略性地利用人类来源的感知优势。

Abstract: When learners receive feedback, what they believe about its source may shape how they engage with it. As AI is used alongside human instructors, understanding these attribution effects is essential for designing effective hybrid AI-human educational systems. We designed a creative coding interface that isolates source attribution while controlling for content: all participants receive identical LLM-generated feedback, but half see it attributed to AI and half to a human teaching assistant (TA). We found two key results. First, perceived feedback source affected engagement: learners in the TA condition spent significantly more time and effort (d = 0.88-1.56) despite receiving identical feedback. Second, perceptions differed: AI-attributed feedback ratings were predicted by prior trust in AI (r = 0.85), while TA-attributed ratings were predicted by perceived genuineness (r = 0.65). These findings suggest that feedback source shapes both engagement and evaluation, with implications for hybrid educational system design.

</details>


### [6] [Situated, Dynamic, and Subjective: Envisioning the Design of Theory-of-Mind-Enabled Everyday AI with Industry Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11342)
*Qiaosi Wang,Jini Kim,Avanita Sharma,Alicia,Lee,Jodi Forlizzi,Hong Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过13次协同设计会议，探讨了心理理论（ToM）能力如何应用于日常AI产品设计，提出了三项设计建议及相关设计张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然心理理论（ToM）被视为人类社交互动的核心能力，且AI领域的ToM能力研究日益增多，但关于这种能力如何转化为日常用户AI产品的设计和体验却知之甚少。研究旨在探索ToM能力如何实际应用于AI产品设计。

Method: 研究者与26名美国AI从业者进行了13次协同设计会议，采用未来展望与反思相结合的方法，共同设想和提炼ToM赋能日常AI产品的设计建议，同时确保建议既具前瞻性又扎根于AI设计和开发实践的现实。

Result: 分析揭示了三个相互关联的设计建议：1）ToM赋能的AI应置于塑造用户心理状态的社会情境中；2）应响应心理状态的动态特性；3）应适应主观个体差异。每个建议中都浮现出设计张力，揭示了从业者设想的ToM赋能AI未来与当前AI设计开发实践现实之间的差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，需要超越静态的、推理驱动的ToM方法，转向将ToM设计为一种支持持续人机交互循环的普遍能力。这些发现为未来ToM赋能AI产品的设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) -- the ability to infer what others are thinking (e.g., intentions) from observable cues -- is traditionally considered fundamental to human social interactions. This has sparked growing efforts in building and benchmarking AI's ToM capability, yet little is known about how such capability could translate into the design and experience of everyday user-facing AI products and services. We conducted 13 co-design sessions with 26 U.S.-based AI practitioners to envision, reflect, and distill design recommendations for ToM-enabled everyday AI products and services that are both future-looking and grounded in the realities of AI design and development practices. Analysis revealed three interrelated design recommendations: ToM-enabled AI should 1) be situated in the social context that shape users' mental states, 2) be responsive to the dynamic nature of mental states, and 3) be attuned to subjective individual differences. We surface design tensions within each recommendation that reveal a broader gap between practitioners' envisioned futures of ToM-enabled AI and the realities of current AI design and development practices. These findings point toward the need to move beyond static, inference-driven approach to ToM and toward designing ToM as a pervasive capability that supports continuous human-AI interaction loops.

</details>


### [7] [Interpretive Cultures: Resonance, randomness, and negotiated meaning for AI-assisted tarot divination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11367)
*Matthew Prock,Ziv Epstein,Hope Schroeder,Amy Smith,Cassandra Lee,Vana Goblot,Farnaz Jahanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨AI在塔罗牌占卜等解释性实践中的应用，分析用户如何寻求、协商和评估"共鸣"解释，并提出支持解释性意义构建的AI设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具在创意和分析任务中日益普及，但在解释性实践中的作用仍不明确。解释性实践中的意义具有主观性、多元性和非因果性，需要研究AI如何支持这类实践。

Method: 通过访谈塔罗牌从业者，结合Hartmut Rosa的共鸣理论，研究用户如何在查询与解释数据之间无因果关系的背景下寻求、协商和评估共鸣解释。

Result: 识别了从业者将AI融入解释工作流程的多种方式：用于应对不确定性和自我怀疑、探索替代视角、简化或扩展现有占卜实践。发现了用户如何评估AI解释的共鸣性。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了AI系统设计建议，以支持解释性意义构建，同时避免消除模糊性或限制用户能动性。强调了在主观解释实践中保持开放性和用户主导的重要性。

Abstract: While generative AI tools are increasingly adopted for creative and analytical tasks, their role in interpretive practices, where meaning is subjective, plural, and non-causal, remains poorly understood. This paper examines AI-assisted tarot reading, a divinatory practice in which users pose a query, draw cards through a randomized process, and ask AI systems to interpret the resulting symbols. Drawing on interviews with tarot practitioners and Hartmut Rosa's Theory of Resonance, we investigate how users seek, negotiate, and evaluate resonant interpretations in a context where no causal relationship exists between the query and the data being interpreted. We identify distinct ways practitioners incorporate AI into their interpretive workflows, including using AI to navigate uncertainty and self-doubt, explore alternative perspectives, and streamline or extend existing divinatory practices. Based on these findings, we offer design recommendations for AI systems that support interpretive meaning-making without collapsing ambiguity or foreclosing user agency.

</details>


### [8] [Data-driven modelling of low-dimensional dynamical structures underlying complex full-body human movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11492)
*Ryota Takamido,Chiharu Suzuki,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 使用神经常微分方程(NODEs)建模非周期性全身运动的低维潜在动力学系统，验证了动力学系统方法可扩展到复杂生态有效的人类运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学系统方法(DSA)在解决人类运动控制的自由度问题方面提供了有价值的见解，但其应用主要局限于周期性或简化的运动。本研究旨在克服这一限制，将DSA扩展到更复杂、生态有效的人类运动。

Method: 采用神经常微分方程(NODEs)建模非周期性全身运动的时间演化作为低维潜在动力学系统。选择棒球投球作为代表性目标运动，验证DSA能否扩展到更复杂、生态有效的人类运动。

Result: 验证实验结果表明，基于NODE的动力学模型能够准确预测复杂投球运动的时间演化(R^2 > 0.45)。值得注意的是，仅使用时间序列前约8%的数据就能解释投球运动后半段约50%的方差，表明后续运动如何从初始条件根据潜在空间中ODE定义的动力学演化。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，将动力学系统方法扩展到更复杂和生态有效的人类运动形式具有潜力，为研究人类运动控制和学习中的自由度问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: One of the central challenges in the study of human motor control and learning is the degrees-of-freedom problem. Although the dynamical systems approach (DSA) has provided valuable insights into addressing this issue, its application has largely been confined to cyclic or simplified motor movements. To overcome this limitation, the present study employs neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) to model the time evolution of non-cyclic full-body movements as a low-dimensional latent dynamical system. Given the temporal complexity full-body kinematic chains, baseball pitching was selected as a representative target movement to examine whether DSA could be extended to more complex, ecologically valid human movements. Results of the verification experiment demonstrated that the time evolution of a complex pitching motion could be accurately predicted (R^2 > 0.45) using the NODE-based dynamical model. Notably, approximately 50% of the variance in the latter half of the pitching motion was explained using only the initial ~8% of the temporal sequence, underscoring how subsequent movement evolves from initial conditions according to ODE-defined dynamics in latent space. These findings indicate the potential to extend the DSA to more complex and ecologically valid forms of human movement.

</details>


### [9] [An Educational Human Machine Interface Providing Request-to-Intervene Trigger and Reason Explanation for Enhancing the Driver's Comprehension of ADS's System Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11507)
*Ryuji Matsuo,Hailong Liu,Toshihiro Hiraoka,Takahiro Wada*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于语音的教育性人机界面，通过提供接管请求触发线索和原因，帮助驾驶员理解自动驾驶系统限制，提高接管安全性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L3级自动驾驶系统在超出运行设计域时会发出接管请求，但复杂交通场景中驾驶员可能同时感知多个潜在触发因素，导致接管时犹豫或困惑。驾驶员需要清晰理解系统限制以确保安全接管。

Method: 提出基于语音的教育性人机界面，在接管请求中提供触发线索和原因说明。通过驾驶模拟器进行组间实验，比较不同HMI条件对驾驶员理解系统限制和接管行为的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示，在接管请求中加入有效的触发线索和原因与驾驶员对系统限制的理解提升相关。大多数使用该方法的参与者能在接管请求失效时主动接管控制，且碰撞次数低于其他HMI条件。

Conclusion: 通过所提方法持续增强驾驶员对自动驾驶系统限制的理解，与更安全、更有效的实时交互相关。基于语音的教育性HMI能改善驾驶员对系统限制的理解，提升接管安全性。

Abstract: Level 3 automated driving systems (ADS) have attracted significant attention and are being commercialized. A level 3 ADS prompts the driver to take control by issuing a request to intervene (RtI) when its operational design domains (ODD) are exceeded. However, complex traffic situations can cause drivers to perceive multiple potential triggers of RtI simultaneously, causing hesitation or confusion during take-over. Therefore, drivers need to clearly understand the ADS's system limitations to ensure safe take-over. This study proposes a voice-based educational human machine interface~(HMI) for providing RtI trigger cues and reason to help drivers understand ADS's system limitations. The results of a between-group experiment using a driving simulator showed that incorporating effective trigger cues and reason into the RtI was related to improved driver comprehension of the ADS's system limitations. Moreover, most participants, instructed via the proposed method, could proactively take over control of the ADS in cases where RtI fails; meanwhile, their number of collisions was lower compared with the other RtI HMI conditions. Therefore, using the proposed method to continually enhance the driver's understanding of the system limitations of ADS through the proposed method is associated with safer and more effective real-time interactions with ADS.

</details>


### [10] [Implications of AI Involvement for Trust in Expert Advisory Workflows Under Epistemic Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11522)
*Dennis Kim,Roya Daneshi,Bruce Draper,Sarath Sreedharan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨AI辅助如何影响用户对人类专家、AI系统及人机组合的信任，通过课程规划任务实验发现专家使用AI的方式会影响用户对其专业能力和信任的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在医学、法律、金融等专业领域的应用日益广泛，需要了解AI参与如何影响用户对人类专家、AI系统及其组合的信任，这对设计有效的人机协作团队至关重要。

Method: 进行用户研究（N=77），采用模拟课程规划任务，比较不同条件下AI的存在与否以及人机协作的具体模式，分析用户对专家专业能力和信任的感知变化。

Result: 虽然顾问制定正确时间表的能力很重要，但用户对专业知识和信任的感知也受到专家如何使用AI助手的影响，这对推荐采纳依赖于用户对推荐者专业能力感知的场景尤为重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果为人机混合团队的设计提供了重要启示，特别是在采纳建议依赖于最终用户对推荐者专业能力感知的情况下，专家使用AI的方式会影响用户对其专业能力和信任的评估。

Abstract: The increasing integration of AI-powered tools into expert workflows, such as medicine, law, and finance, raises a critical question: how does AI involvement influence a user's trust in the human expert, the AI system, and their combination? To investigate this, we conducted a user study (N=77) featuring a simulated course-planning task. We compared various conditions that differed in both the presence of AI and the specific mode of human-AI collaboration. Our results indicate that while the advisor's ability to create a correct schedule is important, the user's perception of expertise and trust is also influenced by how the expert utilized the AI assistant. These findings raise important considerations for the design of human-AI hybrid teams, particularly when the adoption of recommendations depends on the end-user's perception of the recommender's expertise.

</details>


### [11] [Behavioral Indicators of Overreliance During Interaction with Conversational Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11567)
*Chang Liu,Qinyi Zhou,Xinjie Shen,Xingyu Bruce Liu,Tongshuang Wu,Xiang 'Anthony' Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析用户与注入错误信息的LLM交互行为，识别出与过度依赖相关的五种行为模式，为缓解AI过度依赖提供设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的幻觉风险可能导致用户过度依赖AI，而现有方法难以在复杂动态情境中检测这种过度依赖，需要探索用户行为模式与过度依赖的关联。

Method: 收集77名参与者与注入合理错误信息的LLM在三个真实任务中的交互日志，通过语义编码和聚类分析用户交互片段，识别与过度依赖相关的行为模式。

Result: 识别出五种与过度依赖相关的行为模式：低过度依赖用户表现出仔细的任务理解和细粒度导航；高过度依赖用户表现出频繁复制粘贴、跳过初始理解、重复LLM参考、粗略定位以及尽管犹豫仍接受错误信息。

Conclusion: 用户行为模式与LLM过度依赖存在显著关联，研究结果为设计缓解过度依赖的干预措施提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLMs are now embedded in a wide range of everyday scenarios. However, their inherent hallucinations risk hiding misinformation in fluent responses, raising concerns about overreliance on AI. Detecting overreliance is challenging, as it often arises in complex, dynamic contexts and cannot be easily captured by post-hoc task outcomes. In this work, we aim to investigate how users' behavioral patterns correlate with overreliance. We collected interaction logs from 77 participants working with an LLM injected plausible misinformation across three real-world tasks and we assessed overreliance by whether participants detected and corrected these errors. By semantically encoding and clustering segments of user interactions, we identified five behavioral patterns linked to overreliance: users with low overreliance show careful task comprehension and fine-grained navigation; users with high overreliance show frequent copy-paste, skipping initial comprehension, repeated LLM references, coarse locating, and accepting misinformation despite hesitation. We discuss design implications for mitigation.

</details>


### [12] ["I Was Told to Come Back and Share This": Social Media-Based Near-Death Experience Disclosures as Expressions of Spiritual Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11663)
*Yifan Zhao,Yuxin Fang,Yihuan Chen,RAY LC*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了200个TikTok上#nde标签视频，发现用户通过濒死体验叙事表达个人意义，多数包含宗教/灵性主题，这些内容能引发更多互动和深度信仰对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探讨个人如何分享濒死体验，但缺乏对当今社交媒体平台上这些叙事如何被协作传播的研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索TikTok等平台如何促进濒死体验叙事的集体表达和社区互动。

Method: 采用内容分析方法，随机抽样200个带有#nde及相关标签的TikTok视频，分析视频内容和评论区互动模式，特别关注灵性和宗教主题的出现频率及其对用户参与度的影响。

Result: 1. 濒死体验叙事常被用于表达个人意义，大多数帖子包含灵性和宗教主题；2. 这些主题成为用户探索和理解个人灵性观点的手段；3. 包含灵性主题的视频往往能吸引更多用户参与，并在评论区引发更深层次的信仰和意义对话。

Conclusion: 在线平台促进了社区层面的灵性参与，研究结果为设计支持专业社区共享表达和连接的空间提供了启示，表明社交媒体可以成为灵性探索和意义建构的重要场所。

Abstract: People who experienced near-death events often turn to personal expression as a way of processing trauma and articulating beliefs. While scholars have examined how individuals share near-death experiences (NDEs), limited research has explored how these narratives are communicated collaboratively on today's social media platforms. We analyzed 200 randomly sampled TikTok videos tagged with #nde and related hashtags. Content analysis revealed that individuals often use NDE narratives to articulate personal meaning, with spiritual and religious themes appearing in the majority of posts and serving as a means of exploring and making sense of personal spiritual perspectives. Consistent with this, analyses of comment sections reveal that videos containing spiritual themes tend to attract more engagement and foster deeper conversations around faith and meaning. Our findings offer insights into how online platforms facilitate community-level engagement with spirituality, and suggest implications for design of spaces that support shared expression and connection in specialized communities.

</details>


### [13] [Building Intelligent User Interfaces for Human-AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11753)
*Danqing Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出一个参考模型，通过系统化框架分析用户界面如何改善人机对齐，强调UI设计在AI对齐中的关键作用而非实现细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究主要关注训练算法，而用户界面常被忽视或仅视为实现细节。作者认为界面设计是影响对齐效果的关键因素，需要系统化分析框架来填补这一研究空白。

Method: 引入一个参考模型，提供系统化框架分析用户界面在哪些方面以及如何改善人机对齐。通过结构化分类法，结合两个案例研究和包含六个用户界面的初步调查来验证模型。

Result: 参考模型展示了用户界面贡献改善人机对齐的具体途径。案例研究和初步调查验证了模型的有效性，揭示了通过人机交互推进对齐的机会。

Conclusion: 用户界面设计是AI对齐的关键因素而非次要细节。提出的参考模型为系统分析界面对齐贡献提供了框架，为人机交互领域推进AI对齐开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Aligning AI systems with human values fundamentally relies on effective human feedback. While significant research has addressed training algorithms, the role of user interface is often overlooked and only treated as an implementation detail rather than a critical factor of alignment. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a reference model that offers a systematic framework for analyzing where and how user interface contributions can improve human-AI alignment. The structured taxonomy of the reference model is demonstrated through two case studies and a preliminary investigation featuring six user interfaces. This work highlights opportunities to advance alignment through human-computer interaction.

</details>


### [14] [V-SHiNE: A Virtual Smart Home Framework for Explainability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11775)
*Mersedeh Sadeghi,Simon Scholz,Max Unterbusch,Andreas Vogelsang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: V-SHiNE是一个基于浏览器的智能家居模拟框架，用于可扩展且现实地评估解释系统，解决了该领域评估方法困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释对于帮助用户理解和信任自主智能家居决策至关重要，但在该领域评估解释质量和影响仍然存在方法学上的困难。

Method: 开发了V-SHiNE框架：基于浏览器的智能家居模拟环境，允许研究人员配置环境、模拟行为、插入自定义解释引擎，具有灵活的交付模式和丰富的交互日志记录功能。

Result: 通过159名参与者的研究证明了该框架的可行性，提供了一个轻量级、可复现的平台。

Conclusion: V-SHiNE为推进以用户为中心的可解释智能系统评估提供了有效的工具平台。

Abstract: Explanations are essential for helping users interpret and trust autonomous smart-home decisions, yet evaluating their quality and impact remains methodologically difficult in this domain. V-SHiNE addresses this gap: a browser-based smarthome simulation framework for scalable and realistic assessment of explanations. It allows researchers to configure environments, simulate behaviors, and plug in custom explanation engines, with flexible delivery modes and rich interaction logging. A study with 159 participants demonstrates its feasibility. V-SHiNE provides a lightweight, reproducible platform for advancing user-centered evaluation of explainable intelligent systems

</details>


### [15] [Decision Support System for Technology Opportunity Discovery: An Application of the Schwartz Theory of Basic Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11855)
*Ayato Kitadai,Takumi Ito,Yumiko Nagoh,Hiroki Takahashi,Masanori Fujita,Sangjic Lee,Fumiaki Miyahara,Tetsu Natsume,Nariaki Nishino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个结合技术成熟度(TRL)和人类基本价值理论(Schwartz)的决策支持框架，用于早期技术机会发现，通过案例研究验证其可行性并识别成功技术的价值广度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术机会发现方法在早期开发阶段常常未能系统整合终端用户视角，导致技术潜力与市场相关性错位，特别是在消费者需求不明确的情况下。

Method: 提出一个新颖的决策支持框架，整合工程导向的技术成熟度水平(TRL)和Schwartz的人类基本价值理论，通过探索性工作坊分析四种真实技术案例(两个成功、两个失败)。

Result: 发现两个一致模式：1)内部专家比消费者识别出更广泛的价值景观(愿景差距)；2)成功技术展现出更广泛的相关人类价值(价值广度)，表明战略远见可能支撑市场成功。

Conclusion: 该研究为早期研发决策提供了实用工具，建立了价值理论与创新结果之间的理论联系，强调了以价值为中心的评估作为更以人为本的技术机会发现基础的前景。

Abstract: Discovering technology opportunities (TOD) remains a critical challenge for innovation management, especially in early-stage development where consumer needs are often unclear. Existing methods frequently fail to systematically incorporate end-user perspectives, resulting in a misalignment between technological potentials and market relevance. This study proposes a novel decision support framework that bridges this gap by linking technological feasibility with fundamental human values. The framework integrates two distinct lenses: the engineering-based Technology Readiness Levels (TRL) and Schwartz's theory of basic human values. By combining these, the approach enables a structured exploration of how emerging technologies may satisfy diverse user motivations. To illustrate the framework's feasibility and insight potential, we conducted exploratory workshops with general consumers and internal experts at Sony Computer Science Laboratories, Inc., analyzing four real-world technologies (two commercial successes and two failures). Two consistent patterns emerged: (1) internal experts identified a wider value landscape than consumers (vision gap), and (2) successful technologies exhibited a broader range of associated human values (value breadth), suggesting strategic foresight may underpin market success. This study contributes both a practical tool for early-stage R\&D decision-making and a theoretical link between value theory and innovation outcomes. While exploratory in scope, the findings highlight the promise of value-centric evaluation as a foundation for more human-centered technology opportunity discovery.

</details>


### [16] [Who Does What? Archetypes of Roles Assigned to LLMs During Human-AI Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11924)
*Shreya Chappidi,Jatinder Singh,Andra V. Krauze*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了"人-LLM原型"概念，通过文献分析识别出17种人-LLM协作决策模式，并在临床诊断案例中评估不同原型对LLM输出和决策结果的影响，揭示了原型选择对决策系统设计的重要风险与考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在关键领域决策支持中的应用日益增多，需要批判性反思影响人-LLM角色分配和交互的社会技术因素。当前缺乏对人-LLM协作决策中结构化交互模式的系统性分析框架。

Method: 1) 通过范围性文献综述和主题分析113篇LLM支持决策论文，识别并描述17种人-LLM原型；2) 在真实临床诊断案例中评估不同原型对LLM输出和决策结果的影响；3) 分析原型间的权衡和设计选择。

Result: 识别出17种人-LLM交互原型，展示了原型选择能够显著影响LLM输出和决策结果。揭示了原型设计中的关键权衡：决策控制、社会层级、认知强制策略和信息需求等维度。

Conclusion: 人-LLM交互原型的选择对LLM输出和决策结果具有重要影响，为人-AI决策系统设计者提供了关键的风险考量和设计框架。原型选择涉及社会技术权衡，需要系统性地考虑决策控制、层级关系和认知策略等因素。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly supporting decision-making across high-stakes domains, requiring critical reflection on the socio-technical factors that shape how humans and LLMs are assigned roles and interact during human-in-the-loop decision-making. This paper introduces the concept of human-LLM archetypes -- defined as re-curring socio-technical interaction patterns that structure the roles of humans and LLMs in collaborative decision-making. We describe 17 human-LLM archetypes derived from a scoping literature review and thematic analysis of 113 LLM-supported decision-making papers. Then, we evaluate these diverse archetypes across real-world clinical diagnostic cases to examine the potential effects of adopting distinct human-LLM archetypes on LLM outputs and decision outcomes. Finally, we present relevant tradeoffs and design choices across human-LLM archetypes, including decision control, social hierarchies, cognitive forcing strategies, and information requirements. Through our analysis, we show that selection of human-LLM interaction archetype can influence LLM outputs and decisions, bringing important risks and considerations for the designers of human-AI decision-making systems

</details>


### [17] [Wisdom of the LLM Crowd: A Large Scale Benchmark of Multi-Label U.S. Election-Related Harmful Social Media Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11962)
*Qile Wang,Prerana Khatiwada,Carolina Coimbra Vieira,Benjamin E. Bagozzi,Kenneth E. Barner,Matthew Louis Mauriello*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究构建了USE24-XD数据集，包含2024年美国总统选举期间近10万条X平台帖子，使用6个LLM进行多类别有害内容标注，验证了LLM标注的可靠性，并分析了人工标注者人口统计学特征对标注行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选举错误信息和有害政治内容的传播威胁民主完整性，早期检测对理解和缓解其传播至关重要。现有研究缺乏大规模、多类别、时空丰富的有害政治内容数据集，且人工标注成本高昂。

Method: 1) 收集2024年美国总统选举周期内X平台近10万条帖子，包含时空元数据；2) 使用6个大型语言模型对帖子进行五类别系统标注：阴谋论、煽情主义、仇恨言论、猜测、讽刺；3) 通过众包(n=34)验证LLM标注，并与人工标注者进行基准比较；4) 采用群体智慧方法聚合LLM标注，构建稳健的多标签数据集；5) 分析人工标注者人口统计学特征（政治意识形态和党派归属）对标注行为的影响。

Result: 1) LLM与人工标注者具有可比的一致性模式，LLM表现出更高的内部一致性；2) LLM在猜测类别上达到0.90的召回率；3) 60%的帖子至少获得一个标签；4) 人工标注者的人口统计学特征（政治意识形态和党派归属）显著影响有害内容的判断，揭示了判断中的系统性主观来源。

Conclusion: LLM能够以低成本、可扩展的方式有效标注有害政治内容，与人工标注具有可比性。人工标注者的主观性会影响有害内容判断，需要在研究中予以考虑。公开的USE24-XD数据集支持未来关于选举错误信息检测和缓解的研究。

Abstract: The spread of election misinformation and harmful political content conveys misleading narratives and poses a serious threat to democratic integrity. Detecting harmful content at early stages is essential for understanding and potentially mitigating its downstream spread. In this study, we introduce USE24-XD, a large-scale dataset of nearly 100k posts collected from X (formerly Twitter) during the 2024 U.S. presidential election cycle, enriched with spatio-temporal metadata. To substantially reduce the cost of manual annotation while enabling scalable categorization, we employ six large language models (LLMs) to systematically annotate posts across five nuanced categories: Conspiracy, Sensationalism, Hate Speech, Speculation, and Satire. We validate LLM annotations with crowdsourcing (n = 34) and benchmark them against human annotators. Inter-rater reliability analyses show comparable agreement patterns between LLMs and humans, with LLMs exhibiting higher internal consistency and achieving up to 0.90 recall on Speculation. We apply a wisdom-of-the-crowd approach across LLMs to aggregate annotations and curate a robust multi-label dataset. 60% of posts receive at least one label. We further analyze how human annotator demographics, including political ideology and affiliation, shape labeling behavior, highlighting systematic sources of subjectivity in judgments of harmful content. The USE24-XD dataset is publicly released to support future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [SAFuzz: Semantic-Guided Adaptive Fuzzing for LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11209)
*Ziyi Yang,Kalit Inani,Keshav Kabra,Vima Gupta,Anand Padmanabha Iyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SAFuzz是一个基于LLM引导的自适应模糊测试框架，专门用于高效检测AI生成代码中的算法漏洞，通过智能资源分配和动态早期停止机制，在CSES算法问题上显著提升了漏洞检测精度并降低了时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码助手加速软件开发，传统测试框架难以应对AI生成代码的大量涌现。传统模糊测试技术通常采用均匀资源分配，缺乏对算法漏洞模式的语义理解，导致资源使用效率低下且容易遗漏漏洞。

Method: SAFuzz采用混合测试框架，集成了基于提示的行为多样化、带有问题特定预言机的测试工具生成，以及基于LLM的预测器来实现自适应资源分配和动态早期停止。系统通过LLM引导的自适应模糊测试来检测算法漏洞。

Result: 在CSES算法问题上评估SAFuzz，将漏洞区分精度从77.9%提升到85.7%，相比最先进的GreenFuzz减少了1.71倍的时间成本，同时保持相当的召回率。与现有单元测试生成方法结合使用时，将bug检测召回率从67.3%提升到79.5%。

Conclusion: SAFuzz通过LLM引导的自适应模糊测试有效解决了AI生成代码测试的挑战，显著提升了漏洞检测效率和精度，且能与现有测试方法形成互补优势，为AI辅助软件开发提供了更可靠的测试保障。

Abstract: While AI-coding assistants accelerate software development, current testing frameworks struggle to keep pace with the resulting volume of AI-generated code. Traditional fuzzing techniques often allocate resources uniformly and lack semantic awareness of algorithmic vulnerability patterns, leading to inefficient resource usage and missed vulnerabilities. To address these limitations, we present a hybrid testing framework that leverages LLM-guided adaptive fuzzing to detect algorithmic vulnerabilities efficiently. Our system SAFuzz integrates prompt-based behavioral diversification, harness generation with problem-specific oracles, and an LLM-based predictor to enable adaptive resource allocation and dynamic early stopping. Evaluating SAFuzz on CSES algorithmic problems, we improve vulnerability discrimination precision from 77.9% to 85.7% and achieve a 1.71x reduction in time cost compared to SOTA GreenFuzz while maintaining comparable recall. We further observe that combining our approach with existing unit test generation methods yields complementary gains, increasing the bug detection recall from 67.3% to 79.5%.

</details>


### [19] [Patient Digital Twins for Chronic Care: Technical Hurdles, Lessons Learned, and the Road Ahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11223)
*Micheal P. Papazoglou,Bernd J. Krämer,Mira Raheem,Amal Elgammal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 患者医疗数字孪生（PMDT）通过整合多模态数据为慢性病管理提供整体解决方案，早期实施展示了可行性但面临标准化、隐私保护和规模化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性病是全球发病、死亡和医疗成本的主要负担，但现有医疗系统仍呈碎片化且以被动反应为主。需要一种更全面、主动的管理方法。

Method: 采用本体驱动建模和联邦分析试点，基于HL7 FHIR和OMOP标准，构建整合临床、基因组、生活方式和生活质量数据的PMDT系统，通过QUALITOP肿瘤学研究和分布式AI平台进行验证。

Result: 早期实施证实了PMDT的可行性，展示了多模态蓝图自动推理和患者结果预测分析等技术收益，但也暴露出与标准对齐、隐私治理嵌入、联邦查询扩展和临床界面设计等挑战。

Conclusion: PMDT为慢性病管理提供了范式转变，通过DSL和模型驱动工程等机会可推进其发展成为可信赖、自适应的慢性病护理生态系统，需要软件工程师采取具体行动。

Abstract: Chronic diseases constitute the principal burden of morbidity, mortality, and healthcare costs worldwide, yet current health systems remain fragmented and predominantly reactive. Patient Medical Digital Twins (PMDTs) offer a paradigm shift: holistic, continuously updated digital counterparts of patients that integrate clinical, genomic, lifestyle, and quality-of-life data. We report early implementations of PMDTs via ontology-driven modeling and federated analytics pilots. Insights from the QUALITOP oncology study and a distributed AI platform confirm both feasibility and challenges: aligning with HL7 FHIR and OMOP standards, embedding privacy governance, scaling federated queries, and designing intuitive clinician interfaces. We also highlight technical gains, such as automated reasoning over multimodal blueprints and predictive analytics for patient outcomes. By reflecting on these experiences, we outline actionable insights for software engineers and identify opportunities, such as DSLs and model-driven engineering, to advance PMDTs toward trustworthy, adaptive chronic care ecosystems.

</details>


### [20] [Agent-Diff: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Enterprise API Tasks via Code Execution with State-Diff-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11224)
*Hubert M. Pysklo,Artem Zhuravel,Patrick D. Watson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agent-Diff是一个评估智能体LLM在真实世界任务中性能的基准框架，通过沙盒环境访问真实API接口，使用状态差异合约定义任务成功标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM的性能因模型、外部工具访问、提示结构和智能体框架的差异而变化。现有基准需要在控制软件环境变化的沙盒方法和更具生态效度的真实服务方法之间做出权衡，需要一种能结合两者优点的评估框架。

Method: 1. 引入状态差异合约：将过程与结果分离，通过环境状态的预期变化定义任务成功，而非模糊的跟踪或参数匹配。2. 创建标准化沙盒：提供统一的脚本层，让所有模型通过该层执行代码访问外部API（Slack、Box、Linear、Google Calendar）。3. 在沙盒环境中访问真实API接口，同时控制调用、处理和评估的环境。

Result: 使用Agent-Diff框架对9个LLM在224个企业软件工作流任务上进行了基准测试。通过消融实验评估了API文档访问对基准性能的贡献，验证了框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Agent-Diff框架成功结合了沙盒方法的控制性和真实服务方法的生态效度，通过状态差异合约和标准化沙盒实现了对不同智能体LLM在真实世界任务上的公平评估，为智能体LLM性能评估提供了有效的基准工具。

Abstract: We present Agent-Diff, a novel benchmarking framework for evaluating agentic Large Language Models (LLMs) on real-world tasks that execute code via external APIs. Agentic LLM performance varies due to differences in models, external tool access, prompt structures, and agentic frameworks. Benchmarks must make fundamental trade-offs between a sandboxed approach that controls for variation in software environments and more ecologically valid approaches employing real services. Agent-Diff attempts to capture the desirable features of both of these approaches by including access to the real API interfaces for software services while sandboxing the environment in which calls are made, processed, and evaluated. This approach relies on two key innovations. The first is a novel state-diff contract, which separates process from outcome - rather than fuzzy trace or parameter matching, we define task success as whether the expected change in environment state was achieved. The second is a novel sandbox that provides a standardized scripting layer that all models use to execute code against external APIs (Slack, Box, Linear, Google Calendar). Thus, we can evaluate different agentic LLMs against a standardized set of contracts using a unified sandbox while still evaluating their performance on real-world service interfaces. Using the Agent-Diff framework, we provide benchmarks for nine LLMs across 224 tasks utilizing enterprise software workflows. In addition, we evaluate the robustness of the framework with ablation experiments to assess the contribution of access to API documentation on benchmark performance. Code and data: https://github.com/agent-diff-bench/agent-diff.

</details>


### [21] [Improving the Robustness of Large Language Models for Code Tasks via Fine-tuning with Perturbed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11411)
*Yang Liu,Armstrong Foundjem,Xingfang Wu,Heng Li,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过使用扰动数据集微调LLMs，显著提升代码相关任务中对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但会带来轻微性能下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在软件开发中的广泛应用，确保这些模型处理多样化输入时的鲁棒性至关重要，因为输入变化可能导致不正确或不安全的代码输出

Method: 系统评估LLM鲁棒性，通过在字符级、词级和句子级使用扰动数据集微调模型，并与基础模型及使用未扰动数据集微调的模型进行比较

Result: 使用扰动数据集微调显著提升模型鲁棒性（RD通常下降约4%-6%），尤其对鲁棒性相对较弱的模型效果明显；但相比使用未扰动数据集微调，通常会导致轻微性能下降（pass@1通常下降约1%-3%），偶尔观察到性能提升

Conclusion: 使用扰动数据微调LLMs能有效提升代码任务的鲁棒性，代价是轻微性能下降，强调了在代码应用中平衡鲁棒性和性能的重要性

Abstract: Context: In the fast-paced evolution of software development, Large Language Models (LLMs) have become indispensable tools for tasks such as code generation, completion, analysis, and bug fixing. Ensuring the robustness of these models against potential vulnerabilities from handling diverse inputs is critical, as variations in input can lead to incorrect or insecure code outputs.
  Objective: This work aims to improve the robustness of LLMs for coding-related tasks against potential adversarial inputs. Specifically, we investigate how fine-tuning LLMs with perturbed datasets impacts their robustness against input perturbations.
  Method: We systematically evaluated LLM robustness by fine-tuning models using datasets perturbed at character-level, word-level, and sentence-level, comparing results against base models and models fine-tuned on unperturbed datasets.
  Results: Fine-tuning LLMs with perturbed datasets significantly improves model robustness (RD usually drops around 4\% - 6\%), especially for models with relatively weak robustness. However, this fine-tuning process typically results in a slight performance decrease (pass@1 usually drops around 1\% - 3\%) compared to fine-tuning with unperturbed datasets, although occasional performance improvements are observed.
  Conclusion \& Implications: Fine-tuning LLMs for coding tasks with perturbed data effectively enhances their robustness at the cost of a minor performance reduction, emphasizing the importance of balancing the robustness and performance of LLMs for coding applications.

</details>


### [22] [A Grounded Theory of Debugging in Professional Software Engineering Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11435)
*Haolin Li,Michael Coblenz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过扎根理论方法，对专业开发者在真实大型代码库中的调试过程进行定性分析，提出了调试是一种结构化、迭代的诊断过程，开发者通过更新系统心智模型来指导信息收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调试是软件工程中的核心但复杂活动。先前研究记录了调试策略和工具使用，但缺乏理论解释经验丰富的开发者如何在大型真实代码库中推理bug。需要理解专业开发者的实际调试实践，以填补理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 采用扎根理论方法的定性研究。观察了7名专业开发者和5名专业直播编码者在他们自己的代码库中处理17个调试任务，捕捉了多样化的调试情境。通过系统观察和分析构建理论。

Result: 调试被理论化为结构化、迭代的诊断过程，程序员通过更新系统心智模型来指导信息收集。开发者通过交替使用导航和执行策略收集信息，采用前向和后向追踪推理模式，并根据代码库情境、复杂性和熟悉度调整方法。开发者还收集外部资源补充代码证据，经验使他们能够系统构建心智模型。

Conclusion: 贡献了一个基于专业调试实践的扎根理论，揭示了实践中的人本维度，对工具设计和软件工程教育具有重要启示。该理论为理解专业开发者在真实环境中的调试行为提供了框架。

Abstract: Debugging is a central yet complex activity in software engineering. Prior studies have documented debugging strategies and tool usage, but little theory explains how experienced developers reason about bugs in large, real-world codebases. We conducted a qualitative study using a grounded theory approach. We observed seven professional developers and five professional live-coding streamers working on 17 debugging tasks in their own codebases, capturing diverse contexts of debugging. We theorize debugging as a structured, iterative diagnostic process in which programmers update a mental model of the system to guide information gathering. Developers gather information by alternating between navigation and execution strategies, employing forward and backward tracing modes of reasoning and adapting these approaches according to codebase context, complexity, and familiarity. Developers also gather external resources to complement code-based evidence, with their experience enabling them to systematically construct a mental model. We contribute a grounded theory of professional debugging that surfaces the human-centered dimensions of the practice, with implications for tool design and software engineering education.

</details>


### [23] [Addressing OSS Community Managers' Challenges in Contributor Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11447)
*Zixuan Feng,Katie Kimura,Bianca Trinkenreich,Igor Steinmacher,Marco Gerosa,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对开源软件社区管理者面临的贡献者保留挑战，开发了预测性工具和策略来早期识别潜在流失风险，而非仅提供回顾性数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件社区管理者在保留贡献者方面面临重大挑战，现有工具（如仪表板）仅提供回顾性而非预测性见解，无法早期识别潜在流失风险，新解决方案可能反而增加管理者负担而非支持保留管理。

Method: 采用设计科学研究范式，使用混合方法进行问题识别和解决方案设计：通过半结构化访谈、多声文献综述和社区调查识别保留管理挑战；通过迭代构建-评估循环开发和完善诊断流失风险的策略；将策略操作化为基于Web的原型，整合100多名开源从业者的反馈，并在两个开源社区进行实地评估。

Result: 研究提供了：(1) 开源软件贡献者保留管理挑战的实证见解；(2) 支持开源社区管理者保留工作的可操作策略；(3) 未来研究开发或验证开源可持续性理论的实用框架。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统方法解决了开源社区贡献者保留的关键问题，开发了预测性工具和策略，为开源可持续性管理提供了实证基础和实践框架。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) community managers face significant challenges in retaining contributors, as they must monitor activity and engagement while navigating complex dynamics of collaboration. Current tools designed for managing contributor retention (e.g., dashboards) fall short by providing retrospective rather than predictive insights to identify potential disengagement early. Without understanding how to anticipate and prevent disengagement, new solutions risk burdening community managers rather than supporting retention management. Following the Design Science Research paradigm, we employed a mixed-methods approach for problem identification and solution design to address contributor retention. To identify the challenges hindering retention management in OSS, we conducted semi-structured interviews, a multi-vocal literature review, and community surveys. Then through an iterative build-evaluate cycle, we developed and refined strategies for diagnosing retention risks and informing engagement efforts. We operationalized these strategies into a web-based prototype, incorporating feedback from 100+ OSS practitioners, and conducted an in situ evaluation across two OSS communities. Our study offers (1) empirical insights into the challenges of contributor retention management in OSS, (2) actionable strategies that support OSS community managers' retention efforts, and (3) a practical framework for future research in developing or validating theories about OSS sustainability.

</details>


### [24] [Search-Based Quantum Program Testing via Commuting Pauli String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11487)
*Asmar Muqeet,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SB-QOPS是一种基于搜索的量子程序测试方法，通过交换Pauli字符串和测量中心预言机，显著提高了量子软件测试效果，在真实量子计算机上实现了高达100%的故障检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子软件测试方法存在三个主要问题：依赖简单测试输入和统计预言机、需要昂贵的程序规范、在真实量子计算机上的验证有限。这些限制影响了量子软件测试的有效性和实用性。

Method: SB-QOPS作为QOPS的直接扩展，重新定义了基于Pauli字符串的测试用例，引入了利用其交换特性的测量中心预言机。通过基于期望值的适应度函数系统探索搜索空间，采用遗传算法、爬山算法和(1+1)进化算法三种搜索策略，减少了对完整程序规范的需求。

Result: 在IBM、IQM和Quantinuum三个量子计算平台上对最多29个量子比特的电路进行大规模评估。SB-QOPS显著优于QOPS，在模拟和真实噪声条件下，对最多29个量子比特的电路实现了100%的故障检测分数，并展示了跨量子平台的移植性。

Conclusion: SB-QOPS通过搜索方法和Pauli字符串交换特性，有效解决了现有量子软件测试方法的局限性，提高了测试预算利用率，增加了发现细微故障的可能性，为量子软件工程提供了更可靠的测试框架。

Abstract: Quantum software testing is important for reliable quantum software engineering. Despite recent advances, existing quantum software testing approaches rely on simple test inputs and statistical oracles, costly program specifications, and limited validation on real quantum computers. To address these challenges, we propose SB-QOPS, a search-based quantum program testing approach via commuting Pauli strings. SB-QOPS, as a direct extension to a previously proposed QOPS approach, redefines test cases in terms of Pauli strings and introduces a measurement-centric oracle that exploits their commutation properties, enabling effective testing of quantum programs while reducing the need for full program specifications. By systematically exploring the search space through an expectation-value-based fitness function, SB-QOPS improves test budget utilization and increases the likelihood of uncovering subtle faults. We conduct a large-scale empirical evaluation on quantum circuits of up to 29 qubits on real quantum computers and emulators. We assess three search strategies: Genetic Algorithm, Hill Climbing, and the (1+1) Evolutionary Algorithm, and evaluate SB-QOPS under both simulated and real noisy conditions. Experiments span three quantum computing platforms: IBM, IQM, and Quantinuum. Results show that SB-QOPS significantly outperforms QOPS, achieving a fault-detection score of 100% for circuits up to 29 qubits, and demonstrating portability across quantum platforms.

</details>


### [25] [Do Not Treat Code as Natural Language: Implications for Repository-Level Code Generation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11671)
*Minh Le-Anh,Huyen Nguyen,Khanh An Tran,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Bach Le*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Hydra是一个仓库级代码生成框架，通过结构感知索引、依赖感知检索器和混合检索机制，解决了现有RAG方法在代码生成中忽视代码结构和依赖关系的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码大模型在独立代码补全上表现优异，但在仓库级设置中效果下降，因为需要跨文件依赖和结构上下文。现有RAG方法借鉴NLP策略，使用分块索引和基于相似性的检索，导致代码单元间连贯性丢失，忽视结构关系，且相似性驱动方法经常错过功能相关依赖。

Method: Hydra框架包含三个核心组件：(1)结构感知索引策略，将仓库表示为函数、类和变量的层次树，保留代码结构和依赖；(2)轻量级依赖感知检索器，明确识别并检索目标函数所需的真实依赖；(3)混合检索机制，结合依赖感知检索和相似性检索，提供基本构建块和实际使用示例。

Result: 在需要从真实仓库实现函数的DevEval和RepoExec基准测试中，Hydra在所有开源和闭源代码大模型上实现了最先进的性能。该方法在仓库级代码生成中建立了新的SOTA，比最强基线在Pass@1上超过5%，甚至能让较小模型匹配或超越依赖现有检索器的更大模型性能。

Conclusion: Hydra通过将代码视为结构化代码而非自然语言，有效解决了仓库级代码生成中的依赖和结构问题，显著提升了代码大模型在复杂仓库环境中的性能，为代码生成任务提供了更有效的检索增强方法。

Abstract: Large language models for code (CodeLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in standalone code completion and generation, sometimes even surpassing human performance, yet their effectiveness diminishes in repository-level settings where cross-file dependencies and structural context are essential. Existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approaches often borrow strategies from NLP, relying on chunking-based indexing and similarity-based retrieval. Chunking results in the loss of coherence between code units and overlooks structural relationships, while similarity-driven methods frequently miss functionally relevant dependencies such as helper functions, classes, or global variables. To address these limitations, we present Hydra, a repository-level code generation framework that treats code as structured code rather than natural language. Our approach introduces (i) a structure-aware indexing strategy that represents repositories as hierarchical trees of functions, classes, and variables, preserving code structure and dependencies, (ii) a lightweight dependency-aware retriever (DAR) that explicitly identifies and retrieves the true dependencies required by a target function, and (iii) a hybrid retrieval mechanism that combines DAR with similarity-based retrieval to provide both essential building blocks and practical usage examples. Extensive experiments on the challenging DevEval and RepoExec benchmarks, both requiring function implementation from real-world repositories with complex large repository context, show that Hydra achieves state-of-the-art performance across open- and closed-source CodeLLMs. Notably, our method establishes a new state of the art in repository-level code generation, surpassing strongest baseline by over 5% in Pass@1 and even enabling smaller models to match or exceed the performance of much larger ones that rely on existing retrievers.

</details>


### [26] [Beyond Code: Empirical Insights into How Team Dynamics Influence OSS Project Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11692)
*Shashiwadana Nirmani,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Mojtaba Shahin,Xiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究开源软件贡献者如何根据团队动态选择项目，发现沟通相关的团队动态（响应性、语气、回复清晰度）是普遍优先考虑的因素，但重要性因贡献者动机而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源项目推荐系统主要关注技术属性，忽视了影响贡献者加入和留任的协作与社区因素。需要研究团队动态如何影响项目选择，以及这些偏好如何随贡献者动机变化。

Method: 对198名开源软件从业者进行在线调查，结合定量和定性分析，捕捉贡献者对团队动态的感知。

Result: 沟通相关的团队动态（响应性、语气、回复清晰度）在从业者中被一致优先考虑。但团队动态的相对重要性因贡献者动机而异：追求声誉或人脉的从业者更偏好鼓励多样化参与的包容性项目社区。

Conclusion: 理解团队动态如何与贡献者动机匹配，能提供关于从业者项目选择行为的宝贵见解。这些发现可为未来设计更考虑社交协作质量和动机匹配的人类感知项目推荐系统提供参考。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) development relies on effective collaboration among distributed contributors. Yet, current OSS project recommendation systems primarily emphasize technical attributes, overlooking the collaboration and community aspects that influence contributors' decisions to join and remain in projects. This study investigates how team dynamics within OSS communities influence project selection and how these preferences vary across contributors' motivations. We conducted an online survey with 198 OSS practitioners, combining quantitative and qualitative analyses to capture contributors' perceptions of team dynamics. The results reveal that communication-related team dynamics such as responsiveness, tone, and clarity of replies are consistently prioritized across practitioners. However, the relative importance of these team dynamics differs according to contributors' motivations. For instance, practitioners motivated by gaining reputation or networking preferred inclusive project communities that encouraged diverse participation. These findings highlight that understanding how team dynamics align with contributors' motivations provides valuable insights into practitioners' project selection behaviour. Those insights can inform the design of future human-aware project recommendation systems that better account for social collaboration quality and motivational fit.

</details>


### [27] [Leveraging Language Models to Discover Evidence-Based Actions for OSS Sustainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11746)
*Nafiz Imtiaz Khan,Vladimir Filkov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用LLM从软件工程文献中挖掘证据，生成可操作的、有研究支持的推荐（ReACTs），帮助开源项目实现可持续发展


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有模型能预测开源软件可持续性，但这些模型通常提供高层社会技术信号而非具体可操作建议。软件工程研究积累了丰富但未充分利用的实践证据，需要将这些分散的研究发现转化为结构化、基于证据的行动指南。

Method: 设计RAG管道和双层提示策略：第一层系统探索开源LLM和提示技术，从829篇ICSE和FSE论文中提取候选ReACTs；第二层应用后续提示过滤幻觉、提取影响证据、评估合理性和精确度。

Result: 管道生成1,922个ReACTs，其中1,312个通过严格质量标准，按实践导向分类组织，可与APEX等工具的项目信号连接。创建了可重复、可扩展的方法将分散研究发现转化为结构化、基于证据的行动。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了开源可持续性预测模型与具体可操作建议之间的空白，通过LLM挖掘软件工程文献证据，为开源项目提供研究支持的行动指南，帮助项目走向可持续发展。

Abstract: When successful, Open Source Software (OSS) projects create enormous value, but most never reach a sustainable state. Recent work has produced accurate models that forecast OSS sustainability, yet these models rarely tell maintainers what to do: their features are often high-level socio-technical signals that are not directly actionable. Decades of empirical software engineering research have accumulated a large but underused body of evidence on concrete practices that improve project health.
  We close this gap by using LLMs as evidence miners over the SE literature. We design a RAG-pipeline and a two-layer prompting strategy that extract researched actionables (ReACTs): concise, evidence-linked recommendations mapping to specific OSS practices. In the first layer, we systematically explore open LLMs and prompting techniques, selecting the best-performing combination to derive candidate ReACTs from 829 ICSE and FSE papers. In the second layer, we apply follow-up prompting to filter hallucinations, extract impact and evidence, and assess soundness and precision.
  Our pipeline yields 1,922 ReACTs, of which 1,312 pass strict quality criteria and are organized into practice-oriented categories connectable to project signals from tools like APEX. The result is a reproducible, scalable approach turning scattered research findings into structured, evidence-based actions guiding OSS projects toward sustainability.

</details>


### [28] [Verifiable Provenance of Software Artifacts with Zero-Knowledge Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11887)
*Javier Ron,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于零知识虚拟机（zkVM）的可验证源代码溯源新方法，通过在zkVM中执行编译器，同时生成编译输出和密码学证明，验证编译过程使用了声明的源代码和编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可验证源代码溯源在实践中仍具挑战性，最流行的可重现构建技术需要匹配和重新执行构建工具链和环境，过程复杂困难。

Method: 使用零知识虚拟机（zkVM）编译软件，在zkVM中执行编译器，生成编译输出和密码学证明，证明编译过程使用了声明的源代码和编译器。基于RISC Zero zkVM和ChibiCC C编译器实现概念验证。

Result: 在200个合成程序、31个OpenSSL和21个libsodium源文件上评估，证明zk编译适用于实际软件并提供强安全保证：成功阻止所有针对编译器替换、源代码篡改、输出操纵和重放攻击的对抗性测试。

Conclusion: 基于zkVM的编译方法为可验证源代码溯源提供了新颖有效的解决方案，能够提供强安全保证并适用于实际软件环境。

Abstract: Verifying that a compiled binary originates from its claimed source code is a fundamental security requirement, called source code provenance. Achieving verifiable source code provenance in practice remains challenging. The most popular technique, called reproducible builds, requires difficult matching and reexecution of build toolchains and environments. We propose a novel approach to verifiable provenance based on compiling software with zero-knowledge virtual machines (zkVMs). By executing a compiler within a zkVM, our system produces both the compiled output and a cryptographic proof attesting that the compilation was performed on the claimed source code with the claimed compiler. We implement a proof-of-concept implementation using the RISC Zero zkVM and the ChibiCC C compiler, and evaluate it on 200 synthetic programs as well as 31 OpenSSL and 21 libsodium source files. Our results show that zk-compilation is applicable to real-world software and provides strong security guarantees: all adversarial tests targeting compiler substitution, source tampering, output manipulation, and replay attacks are successfully blocked.

</details>


### [29] [Leveraging LLMs to support co-evolution between definitions and instances of textual DSLs: A Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11904)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Regina Hebig,Daniel Strüber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评估大型语言模型在文本领域特定语言语法与实例协同演化中的表现，发现小规模实例效果良好但随规模增大性能下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型驱动工程方法不适用于文本DSL的协同演化，会丢失布局和注释等人机交互信息，需要探索LLM在此领域的潜力

Method: 使用Claude Sonnet 4.5和GPT-5.2对10种案例语言各进行10次运行，评估正确性和人机交互信息保留情况

Result: 小规模实例（修改少于20行）表现优异（精度和召回率≥94%），但随规模增大性能下降：Claude在40行时保持85%召回率，GPT在最大实例上失败；响应时间随实例规模显著增加

Conclusion: LLM在文本DSL协同演化中具有潜力，特别适用于小规模实例；语法演化复杂性和删除粒度比变更类型对性能影响更大，阐明了LLM协同演化的有效应用场景和当前局限

Abstract: Software languages evolve over time for reasons such as feature additions. When grammars evolve, textual instances that originally conformed to them may become outdated. While model-driven engineering provides many techniques for co-evolving models with metamodel changes, these approaches are not designed for textual DSLs and may lose human-relevant information such as layout and comments. This study systematically evaluates the potential of large language models (LLMs) for co-evolving grammars and instances of textual DSLs. Using Claude Sonnet 4.5 and GPT-5.2 across ten case languages with ten runs each, we assess both correctness and preservation of human-oriented information. Results show strong performance on small-scale cases ($\geq$94% precision and recall for instances requiring fewer than 20 modified lines), but performance degraded with scale: Claude maintains 85% recall at 40 lines, while GPT fails on the largest instances. Response time increases substantially with instance size, and grammar evolution complexity and deletion granularity affect performance more than change type. These findings clarify when LLM-based co-evolution is effective and where current limitations remain.

</details>


### [30] [Improving Code Generation via Small Language Model-as-a-judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11911)
*Giuseppe Crupi,Rosalia Tufano,Gabriele Bavota*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现代小型语言模型作为代码正确性判断器，在代码生成任务中表现优于现有RankEF方法，能以低成本实现与大型模型相当的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在代码生成中主要关注主流语言，对特定领域语言支持不足；商业工具依赖大型模型成本高昂；RankEF方法未评估分类准确性，且实验模型较旧，无法确定该方法在现代环境下的有效性

Method: 训练多个最先进的小型语言模型作为代码正确性判断器，评估其区分正确与错误实现的能力；比较这些模型与RankEF的性能差异；分析不使用执行信息时的表现

Result: 现代小型语言模型在代码正确性判断上超越RankEF，即使不利用执行信息；作为代码排序器时，性能提升高于RankEF，能以5-25倍更小的规模实现与大型模型竞争的性能，成本显著降低

Conclusion: 小型语言模型可作为高效、低成本的代码正确性判断器，为企业训练自有代码生成器提供可行方案，性能可与大型商业工具竞争

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in automated code generation. While effective for mainstream languages, they may underperform on less common or domain-specific languages, prompting companies to develop in-house code generators. While open-source models can be trained for this, only LLMs with tens of billions of parameters match the performance of commercial tools, demanding costly training and deployment. Recent work proposed supporting code generation with smaller models (SLMs) by generating multiple candidate solutions and using another SLM to select the most likely correct one. The most recent work in this area is the one by Sun et al. [29] presenting RankEF, a T5 model trained to rank code solutions using both execution-based and non-execution-based information. However, Sun et al. do not assess the T5 ranker's classification accuracy, that is, how often it misjudges correct implementations as incorrect or vice versa, leaving open questions about the reliability of LMs as code correctness judges for other tasks (e.g., automated code review). Moreover, their experiments involve relatively old models, making it unclear the extent to which such a methodology would still help companies in cheaply training their own code generators with performance comparable to those of massive LLMs. We present a study addressing these limitations. We train several state-of-the-art SLMs as code correctness judges and assess their ability to discriminate between correct and wrong implementations. We show that modern SLMs outperform RankEF, even without exploiting execution-based information. When used as code rankers, they achieve higher performance gains than RankEF and perform competitively with LLMs 5-25x larger, at a fraction of the cost.

</details>


### [31] [Studying Quality Improvements Recommended via Manual and Automated Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11925)
*Giuseppe Crupi,Rosalia Tufano,Gabriele Bavota*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究比较人类与AI代码审查的质量改进建议，发现ChatGPT-4能发现更多代码变更建议，但仅能识别10%的人类发现的质量问题，约40%的AI额外建议具有意义，表明两者具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多种基于深度学习的代码审查自动化技术，但这些方法在推荐质量改进方面能否达到人类评审员的水平尚不明确。研究旨在探究人类与AI代码审查的相似性和差异性。

Method: 采用基于挖掘的研究方法：收集240个PR中人类评审员提出的739条代码变更建议，人工分类质量改进类型；然后让ChatGPT-4审查相同PR，比较其与人类评审员推荐的质量改进。

Result: ChatGPT平均推荐代码变更数量比人类多约2.4倍，但仅能发现人类报告的10%的质量问题；约40%的AI额外建议指向有意义的质问题。两者在代码审查中具有互补性。

Conclusion: 当前基于深度学习的代码审查可作为人类审查的补充质量检查，但不能替代人类审查，也不能节省审查时间，因为人类仍需进行手动检查并验证AI报告的质量问题。

Abstract: Several Deep Learning (DL)-based techniques have been proposed to automate code review. Still, it is unclear the extent to which these approaches can recommend quality improvements as a human reviewer. We study the similarities and differences between code reviews performed by humans and those automatically generated by DL models, using ChatGPT-4 as representative of the latter. In particular, we run a mining-based study in which we collect and manually inspect 739 comments posted by human reviewers to suggest code changes in 240 PRs. The manual inspection aims at classifying the type of quality improvement recommended by human reviewers (e.g., rename variable/constant). Then, we ask ChatGPT to perform a code review on the same PRs and we compare the quality improvements it recommends against those suggested by the human reviewers. We show that while, on average, ChatGPT tends to recommend a higher number of code changes as compared to human reviewers (~2.4x more), it can only spot 10% of the quality issues reported by humans. However, ~40% of the additional comments generated by the LLM point to meaningful quality issues. In short, our findings show the complementarity of manual and AI-based code review. This finding suggests that, in its current state, DL-based code review can be used as a further quality check on top of the one performed by humans, but should not be considered as a valid alternative to them nor as a mean to save code review time, since human reviewers would still need to perform their manual inspection while also validating the quality issues reported by the DL-based technique.

</details>


### [32] [An Empirical Study of the Imbalance Issue in Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12038)
*Yuejun Guo,Qiang Hu,Qiang Tang,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过实证研究发现类别不平衡问题是影响深度学习漏洞检测模型性能的关键因素，并评估了现有不平衡解决方案在不同数据集和评估指标上的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在漏洞检测中展现出潜力，但模型性能在不同数据集上表现不稳定。作者推测类别不平衡问题（漏洞代码数量极少）是导致这一现象的核心原因，需要验证这一假设并评估现有不平衡解决方案在漏洞检测领域的适用性。

Method: 使用9个开源数据集和2个最先进的深度学习模型进行全面的实证研究，验证类别不平衡问题的影响。同时评估多种现有不平衡解决方案（如Focal loss、mean false error、class-balanced loss、随机过采样等）在不同数据集和评估指标上的表现。

Result: 研究结果证实了类别不平衡问题是影响漏洞检测模型性能的关键因素。现有不平衡解决方案在不同数据集和评估指标上表现各异：1) Focal loss更适合提高精确率；2) mean false error和class-balanced loss有助于提升召回率；3) 随机过采样有利于提高F1分数。但没有一种解决方案在所有指标上都表现出色。

Conclusion: 类别不平衡问题是深度学习漏洞检测中的核心挑战，现有不平衡解决方案在不同场景下表现不一，需要进一步探索外部影响因素并开发新的解决方案来提升漏洞检测模型的综合性能。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection is crucial to protect software security. Nowadays, deep learning (DL) is the most promising technique to automate this detection task, leveraging its superior ability to extract patterns and representations within extensive code volumes. Despite its promise, DL-based vulnerability detection remains in its early stages, with model performance exhibiting variability across datasets. Drawing insights from other well-explored application areas like computer vision, we conjecture that the imbalance issue (the number of vulnerable code is extremely small) is at the core of the phenomenon. To validate this, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study involving nine open-source datasets and two state-of-the-art DL models. The results confirm our conjecture. We also obtain insightful findings on how existing imbalance solutions perform in vulnerability detection. It turns out that these solutions perform differently as well across datasets and evaluation metrics. Specifically: 1) Focal loss is more suitable to improve the precision, 2) mean false error and class-balanced loss encourages the recall, and 3) random over-sampling facilitates the F1-measure. However, none of them excels across all metrics. To delve deeper, we explore external influences on these solutions and offer insights for developing new solutions.

</details>


### [33] [ModelWisdom: An Integrated Toolkit for TLA+ Model Visualization, Digest and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12058)
*Zhiyong Chen,Jialun Cao,Chang Xu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ModelWisdom：一个结合可视化与大型语言模型的交互式环境，用于提升TLA+模型检查的可解释性和可操作性，解决现有工具在反例解释、状态图理解和模型修复方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLA+模型检查虽然提供强正确性保证，但实践中存在三大挑战：1) 解释反例困难；2) 理解大型状态转移图困难；3) 修复错误模型困难。现有TLA+工具箱的状态图查看器仅提供静态、完全展开的图，缺乏折叠、颜色高亮和语义解释功能，限制了可扩展性和可解释性。

Method: ModelWisdom采用四层方法：1) 模型可视化：提供颜色化违规高亮、从转移链接到TLA+代码的点击穿透、违规状态与破坏属性之间的映射；2) 图优化：包括基于树的结构化和节点/边折叠以管理大型模型；3) 模型摘要：通过大型语言模型总结和解释子图，执行预处理和部分解释；4) 模型修复：提取错误信息并支持迭代调试。

Result: ModelWisdom将原始模型检查器输出转化为交互式、可解释的工作流程，显著提升了对非平凡TLA+规范的理解，减少了调试工作量。提供了网站（https://model-wisdom.pages.dev）和演示视频（https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=plyZo30VShA）。

Conclusion: ModelWisdom通过可视化与LLM技术的结合，有效解决了TLA+模型检查中的可解释性问题，将原始输出转化为可操作的调试工作流，为复杂规范的验证提供了更高效的工具支持。

Abstract: Model checking in TLA+ provides strong correctness guarantees, yet practitioners continue to face significant challenges in interpreting counterexamples, understanding large state-transition graphs, and repairing faulty models. These difficulties stem from the limited explainability of raw model-checker output and the substantial manual effort required to trace violations back to source specifications. Although the TLA+ Toolbox includes a state diagram viewer, it offers only a static, fully expanded graph without folding, color highlighting, or semantic explanations, which limits its scalability and interpretability. We present ModelWisdom, an interactive environment that uses visualization and large language models to make TLA+ model checking more interpretable and actionable. ModelWisdom offers: (i) Model Visualization, with colorized violation highlighting, click-through links from transitions to TLA+ code, and mapping between violating states and broken properties; (ii) Graph Optimization, including tree-based structuring and node/edge folding to manage large models; (iii) Model Digest, which summarizes and explains subgraphs via large language models (LLMs) and performs preprocessing and partial explanations; and (iv) Model Repair, which extracts error information and supports iterative debugging. Together, these capabilities turn raw model-checker output into an interactive, explainable workflow, improving understanding and reducing debugging effort for nontrivial TLA+ specifications. The website to ModelWisdom is available: https://model-wisdom.pages.dev. A demonstrative video can be found at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=plyZo30VShA.

</details>


### [34] [Performance Antipatterns: Angel or Devil for Power Consumption?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12079)
*Alessandro Aneggi,Vincenzo Stoico,Andrea Janes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究实证分析了微服务性能反模式对能耗的影响，发现虽然所有反模式都会降低性能，但只有部分会显著增加能耗，其余因CPU饱和而达到恒定功耗水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能反模式已知会降低微服务系统的响应性，但其对能耗的影响尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在实证探究Smith和Williams定义的广泛研究的性能反模式是否也会对功耗产生负面影响。

Method: 将十种反模式实现为独立的微服务，在受控负载条件下进行评估。收集性能、CPU和DRAM功耗以及资源利用率的同步测量数据，每种反模式进行30次重复运行。

Result: 所有反模式都如预期降低了性能，但只有一部分表现出响应时间与功耗增加之间的统计显著关系。某些反模式达到CPU饱和，无论响应时间如何增加，功耗都保持上限；而其他反模式（如不必要处理、The Ramp）表现出能效-性能耦合，表明存在效率低下问题。

Conclusion: 研究为识别既作为性能反模式又作为能耗反模式的反模式提供了系统基础，并为设计更节能的微服务架构提供了可操作的见解。并非所有性能反模式都会导致能耗增加，某些情况下额外的延迟主要转化为更长的执行时间而非更高的瞬时功耗。

Abstract: Performance antipatterns are known to degrade the responsiveness of microservice-based systems, but their impact on energy consumption remains largely unexplored. This paper empirically investigates whether widely studied performance antipatterns defined by Smith and Williams also negatively influence power usage. We implement ten antipatterns as isolated microservices and evaluate them under controlled load conditions, collecting synchronized measurements of performance, CPU and DRAM power consumption, and resource utilization across 30 repeated runs per antipattern. The results show that while all antipatterns degrade performance as expected, only a subset exhibit a statistically significant relationship between response time and increased power consumption. Specifically, several antipatterns reach CPU saturation, capping power draw regardless of rising response time, whereas others (\eg Unnecessary Processing, The Ramp) demonstrate energy-performance coupling indicative of inefficiency. Our results show that, while all injected performance antipatterns increase response time as expected, only a subset also behaves as clear energy antipatterns, with several cases reaching a nearly constant CPU power level where additional slowdowns mainly translate into longer execution time rather than higher instantaneous power consumption. The study provides a systematic foundation for identifying performance antipatterns that also behave as energy antipatterns and offers actionable insights for designing more energy-efficient microservices architectures.

</details>


### [35] [PPTAM$η$: Energy Aware CI/CD Pipeline for Container Based Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12081)
*Alessandro Aneggi,Xiaozhou Li,Andrea Janes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PPTAMη是一个自动化管道，集成到GitLab CI中，用于测量容器化API系统的功耗和能耗，支持开发人员、测试工程师和研究人员进行版本比较和趋势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于容器的微服务通过快速部署周期演进，但CI/CD管道很少测量能耗，尽管先前研究表明设计模式、代码异味和重构会影响能源效率。需要将能耗测量集成到开发流程中。

Method: PPTAMη是一个自动化管道，集成到GitLab CI中，协调负载生成、容器监控和硬件功率探头，为每个提交收集可比较的指标。它使能耗对开发人员可见，支持测试工程师进行版本比较，并支持研究人员进行趋势分析。

Result: 在JWT认证的API上评估PPTAMη，跨越四个提交收集性能和能耗指标，总结架构、测量方法和验证。系统能够有效集成能耗测量到CI/CD流程中。

Conclusion: PPTAMη成功将功耗和能耗测量集成到GitLab CI管道中，为容器化API系统提供了可比较的能耗指标收集能力，使能源效率成为开发流程的一部分。

Abstract: Modern container-based microservices evolve through rapid deployment cycles, but CI/CD pipelines still rarely measure energy consumption, even though prior work shows that design patterns, code smells and refactorings affect energy efficiency. We present PPTAM$η$, an automated pipeline that integrates power and energy measurement into GitLab CI for containerised API systems, coordinating load generation, container monitoring and hardware power probes to collect comparable metrics at each commit. The pipeline makes energy visible to developers, supports version comparison for test engineers and enables trend analysis for researchers. We evaluate PPTAM$η$ on a JWT-authenticated API across four commits, collecting performance and energy metrics and summarising the architecture, measurement methodology and validation.

</details>


### [36] [Automated Test Suite Enhancement Using Large Language Models with Few-shot Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12256)
*Alex Chudic,Gül Çalıklı*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究few-shot提示中不同测试样例来源（人工、SBST、LLM）对LLM生成单元测试质量的影响，发现人工样例效果最佳，且基于问题描述和代码组合相似度的检索方法最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单元测试生成的传统方法（如SBST）生成的测试缺乏可读性和实用性，而LLM在零样本测试生成方面已有研究，但其few-shot学习潜力尚未充分探索。软件仓库中混合了人工、LLM和传统方法生成的测试，需要研究不同来源样例对LLM生成测试质量的影响。

Method: 使用GPT-4o在HumanEval和ClassEval数据集上进行实验，比较few-shot提示中三种测试样例来源（人工、SBST、LLM）的效果。评估检索方法选择相关样例，包括基于问题描述和代码组合相似度的检索。评估维度包括正确性、覆盖率、可读性、认知复杂度和可维护性。

Result: LLM通过few-shot提示能生成高质量测试，其中人工编写的样例产生最佳覆盖率和正确性。基于问题描述和代码组合相似度选择样例的方法能持续产生最有效的few-shot提示。

Conclusion: few-shot提示能有效提升LLM生成单元测试的质量，人工样例是最佳来源，组合相似度检索是最佳选择方法。这为在混合人机代码库中改进现有测试套件提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Unit testing is essential for verifying the functional correctness of code modules (e.g., classes, methods), but manually writing unit tests is often labor-intensive and time-consuming. Unit tests generated by tools that employ traditional approaches, such as search-based software testing (SBST), lack readability, naturalness, and practical usability. LLMs have recently provided promising results and become integral to developers' daily practices. Consequently, software repositories now include a mix of human-written tests, LLM-generated tests, and those from tools employing traditional approaches such as SBST. While LLMs' zero-shot capabilities have been widely studied, their few-shot learning potential for unit test generation remains underexplored. Few-shot prompting enables LLMs to learn from examples in the prompt, and automatically retrieving such examples could enhance test suites. This paper empirically investigates how few-shot prompting with different test artifact sources, comprising human, SBST, or LLM, affects the quality of LLM-generated unit tests as program comprehension artifacts and their contribution to improving existing test suites by evaluating not only correctness and coverage but also readability, cognitive complexity, and maintainability in hybrid human-AI codebases. We conducted experiments on HumanEval and ClassEval datasets using GPT-4o, which is integrated into GitHub Copilot and widely used among developers. We also assessed retrieval-based methods for selecting relevant examples. Our results show that LLMs can generate high-quality tests via few-shot prompting, with human-written examples producing the best coverage and correctness. Additionally, selecting examples based on the combined similarity of problem description and code consistently yields the most effective few-shot prompts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [37] [Automated Optimization Modeling via a Localizable Error-Driven Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11164)
*Weiting Liu,Han Wu,Yufei Kuang,Xiongwei Han,Tao Zhong,Jianfeng Feng,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIND是一个基于错误驱动学习的自动化优化建模框架，通过局部化错误传播模式和动态监督微调策略优化，解决了现有方法中错误特定问题稀疏和困难问题奖励稀疏的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化优化建模方法在领域特定后训练中存在两个根本局限性：错误特定问题稀疏（L1）和困难问题奖励稀疏（L2），这导致后训练效果受限。

Method: 提出MIND框架，基于优化建模中错误传播的局部化特性，构建高密度训练语料库，并设计动态监督微调策略优化（DFPO）方法，通过局部化精炼处理困难问题。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的实验表明，MIND始终优于所有最先进的自动化优化建模方法。

Conclusion: MIND通过利用优化建模中错误传播的局部化特性，构建了从数据合成到后训练的完整定制化训练框架，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，显著提升了自动化优化建模的性能。

Abstract: Automated optimization modeling via Large Language Models (LLMs) has emerged as a promising approach to assist complex human decision-making. While post-training has become a pivotal technique to enhance LLMs' capabilities in this domain, its effectiveness is severely constrained by the scarcity and underutilization of high-quality training data. However, through a detailed profiling of error patterns across various problem-response pairs drawn from post-training, we identify two fundamental limitations of existing automated optimization modeling approaches: (L1) the sparsity of error-specific problems and (L2) the sparse rewards associated with difficult problems. We demonstrate that these limitations can result in suboptimal performance in domain-specific post-training for LLMs. To tackle the above two limitations, we propose a novel error-driven learning framework -- namely, auto\textbf{m}ated opt\textbf{i}mization modeli\textbf{n}g via a localizable error-\textbf{d}riven perspective (MIND) -- that customizes the whole model training framework from data synthesis to post-training. MIND is based on our key observation of the unique localizable patterns in error propagation of optimization modelings, that is, modeling errors may remain localized to specific semantic segments and do not propagate throughout the entire solution. Thus, in contrast to holistic reasoning tasks such as mathematical proofs, MIND leverages the construction of a focused, high-density training corpus and proposes \textbf{D}ynamic Supervised \textbf{F}ine-Tuning \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (DFPO) to tackle difficult problems through localized refinement. Experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that MIND consistently outperforms all the state-of-the-art automated optimization modeling approaches.

</details>


### [38] [KBVQ-MoE: KLT-guided SVD with Bias-Corrected Vector Quantization for MoE Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11184)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Zhixuan Chen,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KBVQ-MoE：针对MoE模型的超低比特量化框架，通过冗余消除和偏差校正解决VQ量化中的两大障碍，在3比特量化下保持接近FP16基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏专家激活实现了高效计算，但其巨大参数量和内存需求给资源受限环境部署带来挑战。直接应用向量量化(VQ)到MoE会导致性能显著下降，主要因为：1) 专家间冗余表示导致码本容量低效利用；2) 专家聚合放大累积输出偏差，导致分布偏移。

Method: 提出KBVQ-MoE框架，包含两项关键技术：1) 输入驱动的冗余消除：使用KLT引导的SVD提取主导权重分量并在专家间共享；2) 偏差校正的输出稳定：仅对专家特定（非冗余）表示应用向量量化，并通过通道级仿射补偿校正量化输出。

Result: 在各种MoE LLM上的实验表明，KBVQ-MoE显著优于现有量化方法。例如，Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B的3比特量化平均准确率达到67.99，与FP16基线的68.07几乎相同，证明了其在边缘设备等资源受限平台上的高效部署潜力。

Conclusion: KBVQ-MoE通过有效解决MoE模型中VQ量化的两大关键障碍——冗余表示和累积偏差，实现了超低比特量化下的高精度保持，为资源受限环境中的MoE模型部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models have achieved great success by significantly improving performance while maintaining computational efficiency through sparse expert activation. However, their enormous parameter sizes and memory demands pose major challenges for deployment in resource-constrained environments. Vector Quantization (VQ) offers a promising approach for ultra-low-bit compression in Large Language Models (LLMs) by leveraging a codebook, where weight vectors are mapped to the most similar discrete codewords. Yet, directly applying VQ to MoEs often leads to substantial performance degradation due to two critical obstacles: (1) redundant representations among experts cause VQ to repeatedly quantize similar representations for each expert, resulting in inefficient use of limited codebook capacity; and (2) cumulative output bias is amplified by expert aggregation in MoE layers, leading to distributional shifts in the quantized outputs. To address these issues, we propose KBVQ-MoE, a novel VQ framework to enhance extremely low-bit quantization for MoE-based LLMs. KBVQ-MoE integrates two techniques: (1) input-driven redundancy elimination, where a Karhunen-Loeve Transform (KLT) guided singular value decomposition (SVD) extracts dominant weight components and shares them across experts; and (2) bias-corrected output stabilization, where vector quantization is applied only to expert-specific (non-redundant) representations and the quantized outputs are corrected via channel-wise affine compensation. Experiments on various MoE LLMs demonstrate that KBVQ-MoE preserves accuracy substantially better than existing quantization methods. For example, 3-bit quantization of Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B achieves an average accuracy of 67.99, nearly identical to the FP16 baseline of 68.07, underscoring KBVQ-MoE's potential for efficient deployment on edge devices and other resource-constrained platforms.

</details>
